Supported independently by a mechanical diff of the step loop: extracted from both versions with comments stripped and the declared substitutions applied, the bodies compare **equal at 53 statements each**, with exactly two lines added (`t = st->step;` and `st->step = t + 1;`). **(2) The chunked path agrees.** `python tools/cmpchk.py build/tstgld_soft.exe build/tstgld_nat.exe build/tstgld_2c.exe --chunks 1,7,250,100000` gives **`cmpchk [FR-SIM-10 chunk invariance]: 24 passed, 0 failed`** — 3 backends × 2 networks × 4 chunk sizes, each compared against its own unchunked reference run, 36 lines per `path` comparison and 15 per `srext`. Run inside `mingw32-make test`, which exited **0**. **Not proven:** **x86-64 only.** No chunked run has been made on Linux s390x, MVS 3.8j or z/OS, so ACC-5 rows 6 and 7 are *guarded* by this work and not re-established by it — the `onfrun` path they were measured on is unchanged and re-verified, nothing more. The sweep is four values of K, each compared to a common reference rather than pairwise; that is equal to all-pairs only because the comparison is byte equality, which is transitive. `onfinit` has no failing path today, so the `rc` check inside `onfrun` is unexercised. Nothing in Stages 2 to 5 of P-17 exists: there is no stream, no geometry sidecar and no viewer |
| VL-116 | **The chunk-invariance test was shown able to FAIL, on both threats TU-10 names.** A passing test cannot say whether it would have caught the defect it exists for, so on 2026-09-16 two deliberately wrong kernels were built from copies of `engine/src/onfker.c` **in the scratchpad — the tree was never modified** (D-370 declined making this a permanent target). Same platform and compiler as VL-115, **NATIVE backend only**. *(a) Step index restarted per chunk:* `cmpchk … --chunks 7` gives **`0 passed, 2 failed`**, with G-02 moving `5CAB2AA0` → `AE015A00` and G-16 `BAF81D91` → `DE3ED68A`. *(b) Generator re-seeded per chunk:* also **`0 passed, 2 failed`**, G-02 moving `1CFBB679` → `4A328A28` — note that this variant also corrupts the *unchunked* run, which is why its reference differs from the real `5CAB2AA0`. **A third finding, from the first attempt at (a):** restarting `t` without also fixing the carry left `st->step` pinned at K, so the `onfrq1k` driver loop never terminated and the run **hung** instead of producing a wrong answer. That is a true statement about the carry being load-bearing, but a hang is not what `cmpchk` is built to catch, so the control was corrected before being used as evidence. **Not proven:** these are controls on the TEST, not measurements of the shipped engine; they say the comparison discriminates, not that any other defect would be caught. x86-64 NATIVE only, and not re-run on every build |

## Appendix E. Message catalog

| Message | RC | Text | Meaning and action |
|---|---|---|---|
| ONF001I | 0 | NETWORK LOADED N=n E=n CRC=x | Integrity checks passed |
| ONF002I | 0 | RUN MANIFEST … | Start-of-run manifest (NFR-OBS-01) |
| ONF003I | 0 | VERIFY-ONLY: NETWORK OK | Verify-only mode succeeded |
| ONF101E | 12 | MAGIC CONSTANT MISMATCH | Not an ONFLY network, or corrupted |
| ONF102E | 12 | BYTE-ORDER SENTINEL MISMATCH | Transport swapped or translated bytes; re-transfer in binary |
| ONF103E | 12 | UNSUPPORTED FORMAT VERSION v.m | Engine and network versions differ |
| ONF104E | 12 | HEADER CRC MISMATCH | Header corrupted in transport |
| ONF105E | 12 | NETWORK EXCEEDS MEMORY LIMIT | Reduce N, or raise the region |
| ONF106E | 12 | PAYLOAD LENGTH INCONSISTENT | Truncated transfer |
| ONF107E | 12 | PAYLOAD CRC MISMATCH | Payload corrupted in transport |
| ONF108E | 12 | NETWORK DATASET UNREADABLE | The network could not be read at all: absent, empty, or unopenable. Check the ONFNET DD or the supplied path. Added by D-85 |
| ONF109E | 12 | COMPENSATION TABLE INVALID | The v1.1 compensating-input table is present but malformed: its sampled rates are not strictly ascending, the first is not 0, or the rate 0 row is not zero in every neuron. The last of those is checked on the raw bytes, so that ACC-2's guarantee of silence at rate 0 does not rest on the arithmetic under test. Re-emit the network. Added by D-190 |
| ONF201W | 4 | STIMULUS CODE RESERVED, NOT SIMULATED | WATR and BITR are reserved in the MVP |
| ONF202E | 8 | REQUEST FIELD OUT OF RANGE: field | Correct the request |
| ONF203E | 8 | UNKNOWN STIMULUS CODE | Correct the request |
| ONF301I | 0 | REQUEST n COMPLETE FP=x | Request succeeded; fingerprint shown |
| ONF302I | — | STEP SUMMARY: n OK, n WARN, n ERROR | End-of-step summary |
| ONF401E | 8 | CONTROL CARD INVALID AT CARD n | ONFLYDRV request mode |
| ONF901S | 16 | 64-BIT INTEGER SELF-TEST FAILED | Startup known-answer check; compiler or runtime defect |
| ONF902S | 16 | FLOAT SELF-TEST FAILED | Startup known-answer check; float layer defect |
| ONF903S | 16 | NON-FINITE STATE VALUE, REQUEST ABORTED | Engine defect; report with the request |
| ONF904S | 16 | INTEGER WIDTH CHECK FAILED | Platform configuration defect |
| ONF906S | 16 | REQUEST DATASET UNREADABLE OR NOT A MULTIPLE OF 412 | The ONFREQ dataset could not be opened or read, or its length is not a whole number of 412-byte records (IR-JCL-01, IR-JCL-03). Check the ONFREQ DD or the supplied path, and that STEP1 completed. RC 16 rather than 8 so that IR-JCL-04's conditioning stops STEP3: a report must not be printed over a dataset that was never read. Added by D-226 |
| ONF907S | 16 | STREAM DATASET UNWRITABLE | The ONFSTM dataset named by `PARM='STREAM=nnn'` could not be opened or written (IR-STM-01). Check the ONFSTM DD or the supplied path. Added by D-381 |
| ONF908S | 16 | PARM INVALID: text | The PARM begins with `STREAM=` but is not a well-formed `STREAM=nnn` with nnn in 1..999999, or it asks for `STREAM=` and `VERIFY` together (IR-STM-01). The offending text is echoed. **A PARM that does not begin with `STREAM=` is unaffected**: an unrecognised PARM still means SIMULATE, which `tests/run_eng.py` has asserted since D-226 and which this message deliberately does not change. Added by D-381 |
| ONF905S | 16 | REQUEST PROCESSING NOT BUILT AT THIS ENGINE LEVEL | The engine was built without the request loop (D-78). Verify-only runs are unaffected; submit requests against a Phase E engine. Added by D-81. **Kept, by D-227, and still live:** D-221 built the loop, so the shipped engine no longer emits this. The entry is kept because it describes the D-78 engine level, and Phase E has not yet produced an MVS engine, so binaries at that level are not hypothetical. **D-228 then made it reachable again:** TE-09's structural half builds a variant with the request loop compiled out, and that variant reaches this path, so the message is emitted and tested on every run of the suite rather than merely documented. The code is not re-used for anything else |

## Appendix F. Glossary

| Term | Meaning |
|---|---|
| AWS tape image | A file format Hercules uses to emulate a tape volume |
| binary64 | The IEEE 754 64-bit floating-point format ("double precision") |
| CICS | IBM's transaction server for z/OS (IBM trademark) |
| COMMAREA | The communication area passed between CICS programs; here also the batch record layout |
| CSR | Compressed sparse row: a row-pointer array plus a target-index array |
| GRN | Gustatory receptor neuron (taste-sensing neuron) |
| HFP | Hexadecimal floating point, the only floating point in S/370 hardware |
| ICVR | CICS runaway-task interval |
| IND$FILE | File transfer program used over a 3270 terminal session |
| LE | IBM Language Environment, the common runtime absent from MVS 3.8j |
| LIF | Leaky integrate-and-fire neuron model |
| MaleCNS v1.0 | The complete male fruit fly central nervous system connectome (2026) |
| MN9 | A proboscis motor neuron; the feeding readout used by Shiu et al. |
| MVT COBOL | The OS/360 ANS COBOL compiler (IKFCBL00) available on MVS 3.8j |
| PDPCLIB | Public-domain C89 runtime library used with GCCMVS |
| QR TCB | CICS's single quasi-reentrant task control block |
| RENT | Reentrant program attribute |
| SoftFloat / TestFloat | John Hauser's IEEE floating-point software library and its test suite |
| TK5 | MVS 3.8j Tur(n)key 5 distribution by Rob Prins |
| W_syn | Voltage contribution per synapse; the model's free parameter |
